\"content\": \"Why is the sky blue?\"","  }],","  \"stream\": false","}'","```","","See the [API documentation](https://docs.ollama.com/api) for all endpoints.","","### Python","","```","pip install <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Get started</h2><a id="user-content-get-started" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Get started" href="#get-started"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ollama"><pre class="notranslate"><code>ollama
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You'll be prompted to run a model or connect Ollama to your existing agents or applications such as <code>Claude Code</code>, <code>OpenClaw</code>, <code>OpenCode</code> , <code>Codex</code>, <code>Copilot</code>,  and more.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Coding</h3><a id="user-content-coding" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Coding" href="#coding"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To launch a specific integration:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ollama launch claude"><pre class="notranslate"><code>ollama launch claude
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Supported integrations include <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/integrations/claude-code" rel="nofollow">Claude Code</a>, <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/integrations/codex" rel="nofollow">Codex</a>, <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/integrations/copilot-cli" rel="nofollow">Copilot CLI</a>, <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/integrations/droid" rel="nofollow">Droid</a>, and <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/integrations/opencode" rel="nofollow">OpenCode</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">AI assistant</h3><a id="user-content-ai-assistant" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: AI assistant" href="#ai-assistant"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/integrations/openclaw" rel="nofollow">OpenClaw</a> to turn Ollama into a personal AI assistant across WhatsApp, Telegram, Slack, Discord, and more:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ollama launch openclaw"><pre class="notranslate"><code>ollama launch openclaw
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Chat with a model</h3><a id="user-content-chat-with-a-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Chat with a model" href="#chat-with-a-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run and chat with <a href="https://ollama.com/library/gemma4" rel="nofollow">Gemma 4</a>:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ollama run gemma4"><pre class="notranslate"><code>ollama run gemma4
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="https://ollama.com/library" rel="nofollow">ollama.com/library</a> for the full list.</p>
<p dir="auto">See the <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/quickstart" rel="nofollow">quickstart guide</a> for more details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">REST API</h2><a id="user-content-rest-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: REST API" href="#rest-api"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Ollama has a REST API for running and managing models.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl http://localhost:11434/api/chat -d '{
  &quot;model&quot;: &quot;gemma4&quot;,
  &quot;messages&quot;: [{
    &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
    &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Why is the sky blue?&quot;
  }],
  &quot;stream&quot;: false
}'"><pre class="notranslate"><code>curl http://localhost:11434/api/chat -d '{
  "model": "gemma4",
  "messages": [{
    "role": "user",
    "content": "Why is the sky blue?"
  }],
  "stream": false
}'
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">See the <a href="https://docs.ollama.com/api" rel="nofollow">API documentation</a> for all endpoints.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Python</h3><a id="user-content-python" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Python" href="#python"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install ollama"><pre class="notranslate"><code>pip install ollama
</code></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ollama import chat

response = chat(model='gemma4', messages=[
  {
    'role': 'user',
    'content': 'Why is the sky blue?',
  },
])
print(response.message.content)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ollama</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">chat</span>

<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">chat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'gemma4'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
  {
    <span class="pl-s">'role'</span>: <span class="pl-s">'user'</span>,
    <span class="pl-s">'content'</span>: <span class="pl-s">'Why is the sky blue?'</span>,
  },
])
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-c1">message</span>.<span class="pl-c1">content</span>)</pre></div>

<li><a href="https://github.com/ChatGPTNextWeb/ChatGPT-Next-Web">NextChat</a> - Cross-platform ChatGPT UI (<a href="https://docs.nextchat.dev/models/ollama" rel="nofollow">docs</a>)</li>

<li><a href="https://github.com/swuecho/chat">chat</a> - Chat web app for teams</li>
